Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the media framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 488. The resulting operator achieves bound O(362/n^4).

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_488(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 488. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^362(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 219.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on media structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 190. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 80 with relations in degree 3.
